# SGW_All_Classes_Progression_Final_v1

Markdown rendering of `SGW_All_Classes_Progression_Final_v1.xlsx` from the SGW Claude Server Handoff Pack v1.2.
Generated by `xlsx_to_md.py`; cell values only (no formulas, formatting or comments).
Sheet order and headers are preserved verbatim. Evidence labels inside the
sheets control authority, per the pack's `docs/SOURCE_POLICY.md`.

Sheets:

- [00_README](#00readme)
- [01_Dashboard](#01dashboard)
- [02_Branch_Start_End](#02branchstartend)
- [03_All_Final_Nodes](#03allfinalnodes)
- [04_Soldier](#04soldier)
- [05_Commando](#05commando)
- [06_Scientist](#06scientist)
- [07_Archaeologist](#07archaeologist)
- [08_Asgard](#08asgard)
- [09_Free_Jaffa](#09freejaffa)
- [10_Goauld](#10goauld)
- [11_Reserve_Review](#11reservereview)
- [12_Trainer_Server_Export](#12trainerserverexport)
- [13_Progression_Rules](#13progressionrules)
- [14_Sources](#14sources)

## 00_README

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Status | ALL CLASSES FINAL v1 — project progression ready for trainer/server implementation. |
| Coverage | 7 archetypes, 21 branches, 439 purchasable nodes. |
| Historical level cap | 50; contemporary SGW developer coverage described a level cap of 50. |
| Original trainer behavior | Three branches; lower-level skills are prerequisites for higher-level skills; purchases use skill points. |
| Unlock levels | 1 / 5 / 10 / ... / 50 are PROJECT FINAL v1 breakpoints, not recovered original SGW unlock levels. |
| Skill-point cost | 1 point per purchasable node in project v1. Exact original point-gain economy remains unresolved. |
| Prerequisites | Manually curated progression order plus same-lane prerequisites; exact prerequisite graphs are project-authored unless source evidence survives. |
| Raw Training Cost | Original field preserved. Promoted nodes with raw value 0 are visibly flagged VERIFY; not relabeled as original cost 1. |
| Reserve pool | 280 additional candidates remain preserved for later balance/expansion/revision decisions. |
| Soldier | Manual final v1 from the dedicated Soldier pass. |
| Commando | Demolitions source-backed; Stealth/Infiltration and Precision/Marksmanship are strong reconstructed branch labels. |
| Scientist | Medical/Support source-backed; Robotics secondary-evidence strong. Turret-internal attacks are not player tree nodes. |
| Archaeologist | Anthropology/Sociology source-backed; Archaeology secondary-evidence strong. |
| Asgard | Attack/Defensive/Scientific Programs all source-backed. Energy/Gigajoule costs remain partially unresolved. |
| Free Jaffa/Shol'va | Heritage/Tau'ri/Tactics branch names survive in trainer material; membership remains reconstructed. Tau'ri is revision-heavy. |
| Goa'uld | Ashrak/Battle Lord/Servant Lord names survive in trainer material and are strongly corroborated by mechanics/historical descriptions. |

_16 data rows, 2 columns._

## 13_Progression_Rules

| Element | Value | Authority | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| Level cap | 50 | HISTORICAL / EXTERNAL | Contemporary developer reporting described level cap 50. |
| Branches per archetype | 3 | SOURCE-BACKED TUTORIAL | Trainer tutorial explicitly says each character skill tree has three branches. |
| Prerequisite direction | Lower before higher | SOURCE-BACKED TUTORIAL | Higher-level skills require lower-level skills first. |
| Unlock levels | 1,5,10,15,20,25,30,35,40,45,50 | PROJECT FINAL v1 | Exact original breakpoints are not recovered. |
| Skill point cost | 1 per node | PROJECT FINAL v1 | Keeps trainer purchase logic simple until original economy is recovered. |
| Branch points | 0→20 by tier | PROJECT FINAL v1 | Prevents immediate beelining to capstone; not original SGW data. |
| Raw Training Cost | Preserve original field | SOURCE FIELD | Raw 0 is never silently rewritten. |
| Capstone | One per branch at L50 | PROJECT FINAL v1 | Chosen thematically from strongest/most advanced surviving ability candidate. |
| Reserve abilities | Not deleted | PROJECT RULE | All non-core candidates remain available for later revision/expansion. |

_9 data rows, 4 columns._

## 14_Sources

| Source | Authority | What It Supports | Location / URL |
|---|---|---|---|
| Tipps.txt / Trainer tutorial | PRIMARY CLIENT TUTORIAL | Three branches, purchasable skills, skill points, lower-level skills required before higher-level skills. | Conversation/library source |
| CookedDataAbilities.jsonl + CookedDataEffects.jsonl | PRIMARY QA CLIENT | Ability mechanics, Training Cost, effects, monikers, cooldowns and weapon requirements. | Recovered SGW handoff dataset |
| resources.sql trainer topics | LEGACY / SECONDARY | Branch-name support for Jaffa, Goa'uld, Scientist Robotics, Archaeologist Archaeology. | Recovered server/fan-dev database |
| archetype_ability_tree.sql | LEGACY / SECONDARY | Soldier/Commando ordering hints only; not copied as final canonical trees. | Recovered server/fan-dev database |
| VideoGamer SGW interview | EXTERNAL HISTORICAL | Level cap 50 and three specialization paths per archetype. | https://www.videogamer.com/previews/stargate-worlds-interview/ |
| Allakhazam/ZAM GDC coverage | EXTERNAL HISTORICAL | Contemporary class-role and level-cap corroboration. | https://ffxi.allakhazam.com/story.html?mid=120525563521296233&story=12593 |
| Final-v1 progression | PROJECT RECONSTRUCTION | Exact unlock level, branch-point gate, skill-point price, node ordering and prerequisites. | This workbook |
